/* ============ FILTER LOGIC ============ */
function visibleMembers(){
  const q=$('#search').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const prog=$('#fPrograma').value, st=$('#fStatus').value;
  return state.members.filter(m=>{
    if(hiddenNiveis.has(m.nivel)) return false;
    if(prog && m.programa!==prog) return false;
    if(st && m.status!==st) return false;
    if(hiddenLinhas.size){ if(!m.linhas.some(l=>!hiddenLinhas.has(l))) return false; }
    if(q){
      const hay=(m.nome+' '+m.tema+' '+m.linhas.join(' ')+' '+m.programa+' '+(m.origem||'')).toLowerCase();
      if(!hay.includes(q)) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
}
/* reasons: [{type:'linha'|'hub'|'vinculo', label}] entre dois membros específicos.
   'linha' respeita o filtro hiddenLinhas; 'hub' e 'vinculo' não (não são linhas de pesquisa). */
function pairReasons(a,b,includeAux){
  const reasons=[];
  a.linhas.filter(l=>b.linhas.includes(l) && !hiddenLinhas.has(l))
    .forEach(l=>reasons.push({type:'linha',label:l}));
  if(includeAux){
    if(a.hub) reasons.push({type:'hub',label:a.hubMotivo||'Vínculo com o grupo inteiro'});
    if(b.hub) reasons.push({type:'hub',label:b.hubMotivo||'Vínculo com o grupo inteiro'});
    (a.vinculos||[]).filter(v=>v.id===b.id).forEach(v=>reasons.push({type:'vinculo',label:v.motivo||'Vínculo declarado'}));
    (b.vinculos||[]).filter(v=>v.id===a.id).forEach(v=>reasons.push({type:'vinculo',label:v.motivo||'Vínculo declarado'}));
  }
  return reasons;
}
function p135NetworkReasonMatches(r,mode){
  if(!mode||mode==='all')return true;
  const txt=String(r?.label||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase();
  if(mode==='orientacao')return /orienta|orientad|coorient|supervis/.test(txt);
  if(mode==='projetos')return r?.type==='linha'||/projeto|pesquisa|campo|grupo|rede/.test(txt);
  if(mode==='producao')return /artigo|publica|produc|coautor|manuscrit|relat|dado|dataset|capitul|livro/.test(txt);
  return true;
}
function buildLinks(members){
  const includeAux = $('#tHubLinks') ? $('#tHubLinks').checked : true;
  const links=[];
  for(let i=0;i<members.length;i++){
    for(let j=i+1;j<members.length;j++){
      const allReasons=pairReasons(members[i],members[j],includeAux);
      const reasons=allReasons.filter(r=>p135NetworkReasonMatches(r,window.__p135NetworkMode||'all'));
      if(reasons.length){
        const hasLinha=reasons.some(r=>r.type==='linha');
        const w=reasons.reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.type==='linha'?1:0.45),0);
        links.push({source:members[i].id,target:members[j].id,reasons,w,aux:!hasLinha});
      }
    }
  }
  return links;
}

/* ============ RENDER: STATS ============ */
function renderStats(){
  const el=$('#stats'); el.innerHTML='';
  const total=state.members.length;
  const card=(n,l,color,filterFn,active)=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='stat'+(active?' on':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<div class="n">${n}</div><div class="l">${l}</div>${color?`<span class="dot" style="background:${color}"></span>`:''}`;
    if(filterFn) d.onclick=filterFn;
    return d;
  };
  el.appendChild(card(total,'Membros','',()=>{clearFilters()}, false));
  const bolsistas=state.members.filter(m=>m.bolsista).length; if(bolsistas) el.appendChild(card(bolsistas,'Bolsistas','#7437E8',null,false));
  for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(NIVEIS)){
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.nivel===k).length; if(!n) continue;
    const soloOn = hiddenNiveis.size>0 && !hiddenNiveis.has(k);
    el.appendChild(card(n, v.short, v.color, ()=>{ toggleSoloNivel(k) }, soloOn));
  }
  const prazos=[]; state.members.forEach(m=>(m.prazos||[]).forEach(p=>{if(!p.feito){const di=dateInfo(p.data); if(di&&(di.over||di.soon)) prazos.push({m,p,di})}}));
  if(prazos.length){
    const a=document.createElement('div'); a.className='stat alert';
    const over=prazos.filter(x=>x.di.over).length;
    a.innerHTML=`<div class="n" style="color:var(--amber)">${prazos.length}</div><div class="l">Entregas ${over?over+' vencidas, ':''}próximas</div>`;
    a.onclick=()=>switchView('crono');
    el.appendChild(a);
  }
}
function toggleSoloNivel(k){
  const others=Object.keys(NIVEIS).filter(x=>x!==k);
  const isolated = others.every(o=>hiddenNiveis.has(o)) && !hiddenNiveis.has(k);
  if(isolated){ hiddenNiveis.clear(); } else { hiddenNiveis=new Set(others); }
  renderAll();
}

/* ============ RENDER: LEGEND + FILTERS ============ */
function renderLegend(){
  const el=$('#legend'); el.innerHTML='';
  for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(NIVEIS)){
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.nivel===k).length; if(!n) continue;
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='legend-item'+(hiddenNiveis.has(k)?' off':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<span class="sw" style="background:${v.color}"></span>${v.label}<span class="solo">só este</span><span class="c">${n}</span>`;
    d.onclick=(e)=>{
      if(e.target.classList.contains('solo')){ toggleSoloNivel(k); return; }
      hiddenNiveis.has(k)?hiddenNiveis.delete(k):hiddenNiveis.add(k);renderAll();
    };
    el.appendChild(d);
  }
}
function renderLinhaFilters(){
  const el=$('#linhaFilters'); el.innerHTML='';
  const used=state.linhas.filter(l=>state.members.some(m=>m.linhas.includes(l)));
  used.forEach(l=>{
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.linhas.includes(l)).length;
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='filter-item'+(hiddenLinhas.has(l)?' off':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<span class="sw" style="background:${linhaColor(l)}"></span><span style="flex:1;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${l}</span><span class="c">${n}</span>`;
    d.onclick=()=>{hiddenLinhas.has(l)?hiddenLinhas.delete(l):hiddenLinhas.add(l);renderAll()};
    el.appendChild(d);
  });
}
function linhaColor(l){
  let h=0; for(let k=0;k<l.length;k++){h=(h*31+l.charCodeAt(k))>>>0}
  return `hsl(${h%360},50%,55%)`;
}

/* ============ GESTÃO DE LINHAS DE PESQUISA ============ */
function openLinhasModal(){
  if(!isAdmin){ toast('Só o coordenador gerencia as linhas de pesquisa.'); return; }
  renderLinhasMgmt();
  $('#newLinha').value='';
  openOverlay('linhasOverlay');
}
function renderLinhasMgmt(){
  const el=$('#linhasMgmtList'); el.innerHTML='';
  state.linhas.forEach(l=>{
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.linhas.includes(l)).length;
    const row=document.createElement('div'); row.className='id-item'; row.style.cursor='default';
    const sw=document.createElement('span'); sw.className='av'; sw.style.background=linhaColor(l);
    const nameSpan=document.createElement('span'); nameSpan.textContent=l; nameSpan.title='Clique para renomear';
    nameSpan.style.cssText='flex:1;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;cursor:text;border-bottom:1px dashed transparent';
    nameSpan.onmouseenter=()=>nameSpan.style.borderBottomColor='var(--ink-3)';
    nameSpan.onmouseleave=()=>nameSpan.style.borderBottomColor='transparent';
    nameSpan.onclick=()=>startInlineRename(nameSpan,l);
    const count=document.createElement('span'); count.className='lv'; count.textContent=`${n} membro${n===1?'':'s'}`;
    const delBtn=document.createElement('button'); delBtn.type='button'; delBtn.class

async function linkExistingAccess(){
  if(!isAdmin)return;const memberId=$('#linkMember').value,uidv=$('#linkUid').value.trim(),email=$('#linkEmail').value.trim(),m=memberById(memberId);if(!memberId||!uidv){toast('Selecione a pessoa e cole o UID.');return}
  try{await FB().setDoc(FB().doc(window.db,'rede_users',uidv),{memberId,nome:m?.nome||'',email,role:'student',createdAt:FB().serverTimestamp(),createdBy:authUser.uid},{merge:true});$('#linkUid').value='';$('#linkEmail').value='';toast('Conta vinculada.')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Erro ao vincular conta.')}
}
async function unlinkAccess(uidv){if(!isAdmin||uidv===authUser?.uid||!confirm('Desvincular esta conta da plataforma?'))return;try{const u=(state.users||[]).find(x=>x.uid===uidv);const batch=FB().writeBatch(window.db);batch.delete(FB().doc(window.db,'rede_users',uidv));if(u?.memberId)batch.delete(FB().doc(window.db,'rede_member_claims',u.memberId));await batch.commit();toast('Acesso desvinculado. O perfil poderá ser reivindicado novamente.')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Erro ao desvincular.')}}

    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.nivel===k).length; if(!n) continue;
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='legend-item'+(hiddenNiveis.has(k)?' off':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<span class="sw" style="background:${v.color}"></span>${v.label}<span class="solo">só este</span><span class="c">${n}</span>`;
    d.onclick=(e)=>{
      if(e.target.classList.contains('solo')){ toggleSoloNivel(k); return; }
      hiddenNiveis.has(k)?hiddenNiveis.delete(k):hiddenNiveis.add(k);renderAll();
    };
    el.appendChild(d);
  }
}
function renderLinhaFilters(){
  const el=$('#linhaFilters'); el.innerHTML='';
  const used=state.linhas.filter(l=>state.members.some(m=>m.linhas.includes(l)));
  used.forEach(l=>{
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.linhas.includes(l)).length;
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='filter-item'+(hiddenLinhas.has(l)?' off':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<span class="sw" style="background:${linhaColor(l)}"></span><span style="flex:1;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${l}</span><span class="c">${n}</span>`;
    d.onclick=()=>{hiddenLinhas.has(l)?hiddenLinhas.delete(l):hiddenLinhas.add(l);renderAll()};
    el.appendChild(d);
  });
}
function linhaColor(l){
  let h=0; for(let k=0;k<l.length;k++){h=(h*31+l.charCodeAt(k))>>>0}
  return `hsl(${h%360},50%,55%)`;
}

/* ============ GESTÃO DE LINHAS DE PESQUISA ============ */
function openLinhasModal(){
  if(!isAdmin){ toast('Só o coordenador gerencia as linhas de pesquisa.'); return; }
  renderLinhasMgmt();
  $('#
-- pos=344003 --
ated?`A conta foi criada (UID ${created.uid}), mas o vínculo falhou. Vincule o UID abaixo.`:authErrorMessage(e));}
  finally{$('#createAccessBtn').disabled=false}
}
async function linkExistingAccess(){
  if(!isAdmin)return;const memberId=$('#linkMember').value,uidv=$('#linkUid').value.trim(),email=$('#linkEmail').value.trim(),m=memberById(memberId);if(!memberId||!uidv){toast('Selecione a pessoa e cole o UID.');return}
  try{await FB().setDoc(FB().doc(window.db,'rede_users',uidv),{memberId,nome:m?.nome||'',email,role:'student',createdAt:FB().serverTimestamp(),createdBy:authUser.uid},{merge:true});$('#linkUid').value='';$('#linkEmail').value='';toast('Conta vinculada.')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Erro ao vincular conta.')}
}
async function unlinkAccess(uidv){if(!isAdmin||uidv===authUser?.uid||!confirm('Desvincular esta conta da plataforma?'))return;try{const u=(state.users||[]).find(x=>x.uid===uidv);const batch=FB().writeBatch(window.db);batch.delete(FB().doc(window.db,'rede_users',uidv));if(u?.memberId)batch.delete(FB().doc(window.db,'rede_member_claims',u.memberId));await batch.commit();toast('Acesso desvinculado. O perfil poderá ser reivindicado novamente.')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Erro ao desvincular.')}}

/* ============ FILTER LOGIC ============ */
function visibleMembers(){
  const q=$('#search').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const prog=$('#fPrograma').value, st=$('#fStatus').value;
  return state.members.filter(m=>{
    if(hiddenNiveis.has(m.nivel)) return false;
    if(prog && m.programa!==prog) return false;
    if(st && m.status!==st) return false;
    if(hiddenLinhas.size){ if(!m.linhas.some(l=>!hiddenLinhas.has(l))) return false; }
    if(q){
      const hay=(m.nome+' '+m.tema+' '+m.linhas.join(' ')+' '+m.programa+' '+(m.origem||'')).toLowerCase();
      if(!hay.includes(q)) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
}
/* reasons: [{type:'linha'|'hub'|'vinculo', label}] entre dois membros específicos.
   'linha' respeita o filtro hiddenLinhas; 'hub' e 'vinculo' não (não são linhas de pesquisa). */
function pairReasons(a,b,includeAux){
  const reasons=[];
  a.linhas.filter(l=>b.linhas.includes(l) && !hiddenLinhas.has(l))
    .forEach(l=>reasons.push({type:'linha',label:l}));
  if(includeAux){
    if(a.hub) reasons.push({type:'hub',label:a.hubMotivo||'Vínculo com o grupo inteiro'});
    if(b.hub) reasons.push({type:'hub',label:b.hubMotivo||'Vínculo com o grupo inteiro'});
    (a.vinculos||[]).filter(v=>v.id===b.id).forEach(v=>reasons.push({type:'vinculo',label:v.motivo||'Vínculo declarado'}));
    (b.vinculos||[]).filter(v=>v.id===a.id).forEach(v=>reasons.push({type:'vinculo',label:v.motivo||'Vínculo declarado'}));
  }
  return reasons;
}
function p135NetworkReasonMatches(r,mode){
  if(!mode||mode==='all')return true;
  const txt=String(r?.label||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase();
  if(mode==='orientacao')return /orienta|orientad|coorient|supervis/.test(txt);
  if(mode==='projetos')return r?.type==='linha'||/projeto|pesquisa|campo|grupo|rede/.test(txt);
  if(mode==='producao')return /artigo|publica|produc|coautor|manuscrit|relat|dado|dataset|capitul|livro/.test(txt);
  return true;
}
function buildLinks(members){
  const includeAux = $('#tHubLinks') ? $('#tHubLinks').checked : true;
  const links=[];
  for(let i=0;i<members.length;i++){
    for(let j=i+1;j<members.length;j++){
      const allReasons=pairReasons(members[i],members[j],includeAux);
      const reasons=allReasons.filter(r=>p135NetworkReasonMatches(r,window.__p135NetworkMode||'all'));
      if(reasons.length){
        const hasLinha=reasons.some(r=>r.type==='linha');
        const w=reasons.reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.type==='linha'?1:0.45),0);
        links.push({source:members[i].id,target:members[j].id,reasons,w,aux:!hasLinha});
      }
    }
  }
  return links;
}

/* ============ RENDER: STATS ============ */
function renderStats(){
  const el=$('#stats'); el.innerHTML='';
  const total=state.members.length;
  const card=(n,l,color,filterFn,active)=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='stat'+(active?' on':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<div class="n">${n}</div><div class="l">${l}</div>${color?`<span class="dot" style="background:${color}"></span>`:''}`;
    if(filterFn) d.onclick=filterFn;
    return d;
  };
  el.appendChild(card(total,'Membros','',()=>{clearFilters()}, false));
  const bolsistas=state.members.filter(m=>m.bolsista).length; if(bolsistas) el.appendChild(card(bolsistas,'Bolsistas','#7437E8',null,false));
  for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(NIVEIS)){
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.nivel===k).length; if(!n) continue;
    const soloOn = hiddenNiveis.size>0 && !hiddenNiveis.has(k);
    el.appendChild(card(n, v.short, v.color, ()=>{ toggleSoloNivel(k) }, soloOn));
  }
  const prazos=[]; state.members.forEach(m=>(m.prazos||[]).forEach(p=>{if(!p.feito){const di=dateInfo(p.data); if(di&&(di.over||di.soon)) prazos.push({m,p,di})}}));
  if(prazos.length){
    const a=document.createElement('div'); a.className='stat alert';
    const over=prazos.filter(x=>x.di.over).length;
    a.innerHTML=`<div class="n" style="color:var(--amber)">${prazos.length}</div><div class="l">Entregas ${over?over+' vencidas, ':''}próximas</div>`;
    a.onclick=()=>switchView('crono');
    el.appendChild(a);
  }
}
function toggleSoloNivel(k){
  const others=Object.keys(NIVEIS).filter(x=>x!==k);
  const isolated = others.every(o=>hiddenNiveis.has(o)) && !hiddenNiveis.has(k);
  if(isolated){ hiddenNiveis.clear(); } else { hiddenNiveis=new Set(others); }
  renderAll();
}

/* ============ RENDER: LEGEND + FILTERS ============ */
function renderLegend(){
  const el=$('#legend'); el.innerHTML='';
  for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(NIVEIS)){
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.nivel===k).length; if(!n) continue;
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='legend-item'+(hiddenNiveis.has(k)?' off':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<span class="sw" style="background:${v.color}"></span>${v.label}<span class="solo">só este</span><span class="c">${n}</span>`;
    d.onclick=(e)=>{
      if(e.target.classList.contains('solo')){ toggleSoloNivel(k); return; }
      hiddenNiveis.has(k)?hiddenNiveis.delete(k):hiddenNiveis.add(k);renderAll();
    };
    el.appendChild(d);
  }
}
function renderLinhaFilters(){
  const el=$('#linhaFilters'); el.innerHTML='';
  const used=state.linhas.filter(l=>state.members.some(m=>m.linhas.includes(l)));
  used.forEach(l=>{
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.linhas.includes(l)).length;
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='filter-item'+(hiddenLinhas.has(l)?' off':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<span class="sw" style="background:${linhaColor(l)}"></span><span style="flex:1;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${l}</span><span class="c">${n}</span>`;
    d.onclick=()=>{hiddenLinhas.has(l)?hiddenLinhas.delete(l):hiddenLinhas.add(l);renderAll()};
    el.appendChild(d);
  });
}
function linhaColor(l){
  let h=0; for(let k=0;k<l.length;k++){h=(h*31+l.charCodeAt(k))>>>0}
  return `hsl(${h%360},50%,55%)`;
}

    el.appendChild(card(n, v.short, v.color, ()=>{ toggleSoloNivel(k) }, soloOn));
  }
  const prazos=[]; state.members.forEach(m=>(m.prazos||[]).forEach(p=>{if(!p.feito){const di=dateInfo(p.data); if(di&&(di.over||di.soon)) prazos.push({m,p,di})}}));
  if(prazos.length){
    const a=document.createElement('div'); a.className='stat alert';
    const over=prazos.filter(x=>x.di.over).length;
    a.innerHTML=`<div class="n" style="color:var(--amber)">${prazos.length}</div><div class="l">Entregas ${over?over+' vencidas, ':''}próximas</div>`;
    a.onclick=()=>switchView('crono');
    el.appendChild(a);
  }
}
function toggleSoloNivel(k){
  const others=Object.keys(NIVEIS).filter(x=>x!==k);
  const isolated = others.every(o=>hiddenNiveis.has(o)) && !hiddenNiveis.has(k);
  if(isolated){ hiddenNiveis.clear(); } else { hiddenNiveis=new Set(others); }
  renderAll();
}

/* ============ RENDER: LEGEND + FILTERS ============ */
function renderLegend(){
  const el=$('#legend'); el.innerHTML='';
  for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(NIVEIS)){
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.nivel===k).length; if(!n) continue;
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='legend-item'+(hiddenNiveis.has(k)?' off':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<span class="sw" style="background:${v.color}"></span>${v.label}<span class="solo">só este</span><span class="c">${n}</span>`;
    d.onclick=(e)=>{
      if(e.target.classList.contains('solo')){ toggleSoloNivel(k); return; }
      hiddenNiveis.has(k)?hiddenNiveis.delete(k):hiddenNiveis.add(k);renderAll();
    };
    el.appendChild(d);
  }
}
function renderLinhaFilters(){
  const el=$('#linhaFilters'); el.innerHTML='';
  const used=state.linhas.filter(l=>state.members.some(m=>m.linhas.includes(l)));
  used.forEach(l=>{
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.linhas.includes(l)).length;
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='filter-item'+(hiddenLinhas.has(l)?' off':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<span class="sw" style="background:${linhaColor(l)}"></span><span style="flex:1;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${l}</span><span class="c">${n}</span>`;
    d.onclick=()=>{hiddenLinhas.has(l)?hiddenLinhas.delete(l):hiddenLinhas.add(l);renderAll()};
    el.appendChild(d);
  });
}
function linhaColor(l){
  let h=0; for(let k=0;k<l.length;k++){h=(h*31+l.charCodeAt(k))>>>0}
  return `hsl(${h%360},50%,55%)`;
}

  renderLinhasMgmt();
  $('#newLinha').value='';
  openOverlay('linhasOverlay');
}

  state.linhas.forEach(l=>{
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.linhas.includes(l)).length;
    const row=document.create

async function unlinkAccess(uidv){if(!isAdmin||uidv===authUser?.uid||!confirm('Desvincular esta conta da plataforma?'))return;try{const u=(state.users||[]).find(x=>x.uid===uidv);const batch=FB().writeBatch(window.db);batch.delete(FB().doc(window.db,'rede_users',uidv));if(u?.memberId)batch.delete(FB().doc(window.db,'rede_member_claims',u.memberId));await batch.commit();toast('Acesso desvinculado. O perfil poderá ser reivindicado novamente.')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Erro ao desvincular.')}}

/* ============ FILTER LOGIC ============ */
function visibleMembers(){
  const q=$('#search').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const prog=$('#fPrograma').value, st=$('#fStatus').value;
  return state.members.filter(m=>{
    if(hiddenNiveis.has(m.nivel)) return false;
    if(prog && m.programa!==prog) return false;
    if(st && m.status!==st) return false;
    if(hiddenLinhas.size){ if(!m.linhas.some(l=>!hiddenLinhas.has(l))) return false; }
    if(q){
      const hay=(m.nome+' '+m.tema+' '+m.linhas.join(' ')+' '+m.programa+' '+(m.origem||'')).toLowerCase();
      if(!hay.includes(q)) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
}
/* reasons: [{type:'linha'|'hub'|'vinculo', label}] entre dois membros específicos.
   'linha' respeita o filtro hiddenLinhas; 'hub' e 'vinculo' não (não são linhas de pesquisa). */
function pairReasons(a,b,includeAux){
  const reasons=[];
  a.linhas.filter(l=>b.linhas.includes(l) && !hiddenLinhas.has(l))
    .forEach(l=>reasons.push({type:'linha',label:l}));
  if(includeAux){
    if(a.hub) reasons.push({type:'hub',label:a.hubMotivo||'Vínculo com o grupo inteiro'});
    if(b.hub) reasons.push({type:'hub',label:b.hubMotivo||'Vínculo com o grupo inteiro'});
    (a.vinculos||[]).filter(v=>v.id===b.id).forEach(v=>reasons.push({type:'vinculo',label:v.motivo||'Vínculo declarado'}));
    (b.vinculos||[]).filter(v=>v.id===a.id).forEach(v=>reasons.push({type:'vinculo',label:v.motivo||'Vínculo declarado'}));
  }
  return reasons;
}
function p135NetworkReasonMatches(r,mode){
  if(!mode||mode==='all')return true;
  const txt=String(r?.label||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase();
  if(mode==='orientacao')return /orienta|orientad|coorient|supervis/.test(txt);
  if(mode==='projetos')return r?.type==='linha'||/projeto|pesquisa|campo|grupo|rede/.test(txt);
  if(mode==='producao')return /artigo|publica|produc|coautor|manuscrit|relat|dado|dataset|capitul|livro/.test(txt);
  return true;
}
function buildLinks(members){
  const includeAux = $('#tHubLinks') ? $('#tHubLinks').checked : true;
  const links=[];
  for(let i=0;i<members.length;i++){
    for(let j=i+1;j<members.length;j++){
      const allReasons=pairReasons(members[i],members[j],includeAux);
      const reasons=allReasons.filter(r=>p135NetworkReasonMatches(r,window.__p135NetworkMode||'all'));
      if(reasons.length){
        const hasLinha=reasons.some(r=>r.type==='linha');
        const w=reasons.reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.type==='linha'?1:0.45),0);
        links.push({source:members[i].id,target:members[j].id,reasons,w,aux:!hasLinha});
      }
    }
  }
  return links;
}

/* ============ RENDER: STATS ============ */
function renderStats(){
  const el=$('#stats'); el.innerHTML='';
  const total=state.members.length;
  const card=(n,l,color,filterFn,active)=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='stat'+(active?' on':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<div class="n">${n}</div><div class="l">${l}</div>${color?`<span class="dot" style="background:${color}"></span>`:''}`;
    if(filterFn) d.onclick=filterFn;
    return d;
  };
  el.appendChild(card(total,'Membros','',()=>{clearFilters()}, false));
  const bolsistas=state.members.filter(m=>m.bolsista).length; if(bolsistas) el.appendChild(card(bolsistas,'Bolsistas','#7437E8',null,false));
  for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(NIVEIS)){
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.nivel===k).length; if(!n) continue;
    const soloOn = hiddenNiveis.size>0 && !hiddenNiveis.has(k);
    el.appendChild(card(n, v.short, v.color, ()=>{ toggleSoloNivel(k) }, soloOn));
  }
  const prazos=[]; state.members.forEach(m=>(m.prazos||[]).forEach(p=>{if(!p.feito){const di=dateInfo(p.data); if(di&&(di.over||di.soon)) prazos.push({m,p,di})}}));
  if(prazos.length){
    const a=document.createElement('div'); a.className='stat alert';
    const over=prazos.filter(x=>x.di.over).length;
    a.innerHTML=`<div class="n" style="color:var(--amber)">${prazos.length}</div><div class="l">Entregas ${over?over+' vencidas, ':''}próximas</div>`;
    a.onclick=()=>switchView('crono');
    el.appendChild(a);
  }
}
function toggleSoloNivel(k){
  const others=Object.keys(NIVEIS).filter(x=>x!==k);

  if(isolated){ hiddenNiveis.clear(); } else { hiddenNiveis=new Set(others); }
  renderAll();

      const reasons=allReasons.filter(r=>p135NetworkReasonMatches(r,window.__p135NetworkMode||'all'));
      if(reasons.length){
        const hasLinha=reasons.some(r=>r.type==='linha');
        const w=reasons.reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.type==='linha'?1:0.45),0);
        links.push({source:members[i].id,target:members[j].id,reasons,w,aux:!hasLinha});
      }
    }
  }
  return links;
}

/* ============ RENDER: STATS ============ */
function renderStats(){
  const el=$('#stats'); el.innerHTML='';
  const total=state.members.length;
  const card=(n,l,color,filterFn,active)=>{
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='stat'+(active?' on':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<div class="n">${n}</div><div class="l">${l}</div>${color?`<span class="dot" style="background:${color}"></span>`:''}`;
    if(filterFn) d.onclick=filterFn;
    return d;
  };
  el.appendChild(card(total,'Membros','',()=>{clearFilters()}, false));
  const bolsistas=state.members.filter(m=>m.bolsista).length; if(bolsistas) el.appendChild(card(bolsistas,'Bolsistas','#7437E8',null,false));
  for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(NIVEIS)){
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.nivel===k).length; if(!n) continue;
    const soloOn = hiddenNiveis.size>0 && !hiddenNiveis.has(k);
    el.appendChild(card(n, v.short, v.color, ()=>{ toggleSoloNivel(k) }, soloOn));
  }
  const prazos=[]; state.members.forEach(m=>(m.prazos||[]).forEach(p=>{if(!p.feito){const di=dateInfo(p.data); if(di&&(di.over||di.soon)) prazos.push({m,p,di})}}));
  if(prazos.length){
    const a=document.createElement('div'); a.className='stat alert';
    const over=prazos.filter(x=>x.di.over).length;
    a.innerHTML=`<div class="n" style="color:var(--amber)">${prazos.length}</div><div class="l">Entregas ${over?over+' vencidas, ':''}próximas</div>`;
    a.onclick=()=>switchView('crono');
    el.appendChild(a);
  }
}
function toggleSoloNivel(k){
  const others=Object.keys(NIVEIS).filter(x=>x!==k);
  const isolated = others.every(o=>hiddenNiveis.has(o)) && !hiddenNiveis.has(k);
  if(isolated){ hiddenNiveis.clear(); } else { hiddenNiveis=new Set(others); }
  renderAll();
}

/* ============ RENDER: LEGEND + FILTERS ============ */
function renderLegend(){
  const el=$('#legend'); el.innerHTML='';
  for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(NIVEIS)){
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.nivel===k).length; if(!n) continue;
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='legend-item'+(hiddenNiveis.has(k)?' off':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<span class="sw" style="background:${v.color}"></span>${v.label}<span class="solo">só este</span><span class="c">${n}</span>`;
    d.onclick=(e)=>{
      if(e.target.classList.contains('solo')){ toggleSoloNivel(k); return; }
      hiddenNiveis.has(k)?hiddenNiveis.delete(k):hiddenNiveis.add(k);renderAll();
    };
    el.appendChild(d);
  }
}
function renderLinhaFilters(){
  const el=$('#linhaFilters'); el.innerHTML='';
  const used=state.linhas.filter(l=>state.members.some(m=>m.linhas.includes(l)));
  used.forEach(l=>{
    const n=state.members.filter(m=>m.linhas.includes(l)).length;
    const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='filter-item'+(hiddenLinhas.has(l)?' off':'');
    d.innerHTML=`<span class="sw" style="background:${linhaColor(l)}"></span><span style="flex:1;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${l}</span><span class="c">${n}</span>`;
    d.onclick=()=>{hiddenLinhas.has(l)?hiddenLinhas.delete(l):hiddenLinhas.add(l);renderAll()};
    el.appendChild(d);
  });
}
function linhaColor(l){
  let h=0; for(let k=0;k<l.length;k++){h=(h*31+l.charCodeAt(k))>>>0}
  return `hsl(${h%360},50%,55%)`;
}

function memberRepositoryItems(memberId){
  const out=[];
  compactPublicationsForDisplay(state.publicacoes.filter(p=>p.memberId===memberId)).forEach(p=>out.push({source:'publicacao',id:p.id,title:p.titulo||p.fileName||'Documento',kind:(CATEGORIAS[repoCategoryKey(p.categoria)]?.label||p.categoriaOutro||p.categoria||'Publicação'),url:p.url||'',fileName:p.fileName||'',isFile:p.tipo==='arquivo',ts:p.ts,reviewFlow:!!p.reviewFlow,reviewVersion:p.reviewVersion||0,reviewStatus:p.reviewStatus||'',reviewReviewerId:p.reviewReviewerId||'',reviewReturnToId:p.reviewReturnToId||'',reviewNextRecipientId:p.reviewNextRecipientId||'',reviewNote:p.reviewNote||'',onlineEditVersion:p.onlineEditVersion||1,raw:p}));
  state.repositoryPackages.filter(p=>p.ownerMemberId===memberId).forEach(p=>out.push({source:'package',id:p.id,title:p.title||'Conjunto de arquivos',kind:'Dossiê · '+repoPurposeLabel(p),url:'',fileName:'',isFile:false,ts:p.ts,package:p}));
  state.activities.filter(a=>a.ownerId===memberId && (a.fileUrl||a.externalUrl)).forEach(a=>out.push({source:'atividade',id:a.id,title:a.title||'Registro de acompanhamento',kind:ACTIVITY_TYPES[a.type]||a.type||'Acompanhamento',url:a.fileUrl||a.externalUrl||'',fileName:a.fileName||'',isFile:!!a.fileUrl,ts:a.updatedAt||a.createdAt}));
  const m=memberById(memberId); (m?.resultados||[]).forEach((r,i)=>{if(r.url)out.push({source:'resultado',id:'r'+i,title:r.titulo||'Resultado / produto',kind:'Resultado / produto',url:r.url,fileName:'',isFile:false,ts:null})});
  return out.sort((a,b)=>{const ta=a.ts?.toMillis?a.ts.toMillis():0,tb=b.ts?.toMillis?b.ts.toMillis():0;return tb-ta});
}

function renderMemberRepository(){
  const m=memberById(repoMemberId); if(!m||!$('#repoList'))return;
  $('#repoTitle').textContent='Repositório · '+m.nome;
  $('#repoScholar').innerHTML=scholarshipHTML(m);
  const list=memberRepositoryItems(m.id); $('#repoCount').textContent=list.length;
  const canAdd=isAdmin||isMe(m.id); $('#repoAddBtn').style.display=canAdd?'inline-flex':'none';
  $('#repoList').innerHTML=list.length?list.map(it=>{
    const icon=it.reviewFlow?'✍️':(it.isFile?'📄':'🔗');
    const view=it.source==='package'?`<button class="btn ghost" type="button" onclick="openRepositoryPackage('${it.id}')">📦 Abrir dossiê</button>`:it.raw?.labRun?`<button class="btn primary" type="button" onclick="openLabRun('${it.raw.labRunId}')">📈 Resultados</button>`:it.isFile?`<button class="btn ghost" type="button" onclick="openFilePreview(decodeURIComponent('${encodeURIComponent(it.url)}'),decodeURIComponent('${encodeURIComponent(it.fileName||it.title||'Arquivo')}'),{source:'publication',id:'${it.id}'})">Visualizar</button><button class="btn review-download" type="button" onclick="downloadRepoFile(decodeURIComponent('${encodeURIComponent(it.url)}'),decodeURIComponent('${encodeURIComponent(it.fileName||it.title||'Arquivo')}'))">Baixar</button>`:`<a class="btn ghost" href="${esc(it.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Abrir</a>`;
    const rev=it.reviewFlow?reviewBadgeHTML(it.raw):'';
    const online=it.source==='publicacao'?officeActionButtons(it.raw):'';
    const chat=it.source==='publicacao'&&it.isFile?repositoryCommunicationButtons(it.raw):'';
    const respond=it.reviewFlow&&canActOnReview(it.raw)?`<button class="btn review-action" type="button" onclick="openReviewResponse('${it.id}')">${reviewActionLabel(it.raw)}</button>`:'';
    const ver=!it.reviewFlow&&it.onlineEditVersion>1?`<span class="office-version">✏️ edição v${it.onlineEditVersion}</span>`:'';
    return `<div class="repo-item"><div class="repo-icon">${icon}</div><div class="repo-main"><div class="repo-title">${esc(it.title)}</div><div class="repo-meta">${esc(it.kind)}</div>${ver}${rev}${it.reviewNote?`<div class="review-note">💬 ${esc(it.reviewNote)}</div>`:''}${it.raw?scientificContextHTML(it.raw):''}</div><div class="repo-actions">${view}${it.raw?labRepoButton(it.raw):''}${online}${chat}${respond}</div></div>`
  }).join(''):'<div class="repo-empty">Ainda não há documentos ou links registrados para esta pessoa.</div>';
}
function fmtTime(ts){
  if(!ts) return '';
  const d=new Date(ts), now=new Date();
  const sameDay=d.toDateString()===now.toDateString();
  return sameDay ? d.toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})
                 : d.toLocaleDateString('pt-BR',{day:'2-digit',month:'2-digit'})+' '+d.toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
}
function dayLabel(ts){
  const d=new Date(ts), now=new Date();
  const y=new Date(now); y.setDate(now.getDate()-1);
  if(d.toDateString()===now.toDateString()) return 'Hoje';
  if(d.toDateString()===y.toDateString()) return 'Ontem';
  return d.toLocaleDateString('pt-BR',{day:'2-digit',month:'long',year:'numeric'});
}

let dbReady = false;
function waitFirebase(){
  return new Promise(res=>{
    if(window.db && window.fbFns){dbReady=true;return res()}
    window.addEventListener('firebase-ready',()=>{dbReady=true;res()},{once:true});
  });
}

function renderDetail(){

    </div>`;
}

    ${m.notas?`<div class="dsec"><div class="lab">Notas</div><p>${esc(m.notas)}</p></div>`:''}
    <div class="dsec"><div class="lab">Trabalha com (${neigh.length})</div><div class="neighbors">${neigh.slice(0,10).map(x=>`<div class="nb" onclick="selectNode('${x.o.id}')"><span class="d" style="background:${NIVEIS[x.o.nivel].color}"></span>${esc(x.o.nome)}<span class="via">${esc(x.shared[0])}${x.shared.length>1?' +'+(x.shared.length-1):''}</span></div>`).join('')||'<span style="color:var(--ink-3);font-size:13px">Sem ligações pelas linhas atuais.</span>'}</div></div>
    <div class="dactions">
      ${editable?`<button class="btn primary" style="flex:1" onclick="openMember('${m.id}')">Editar</button>`:`<button class="btn" style="flex:1" disabled title="Só ${esc(m.nome)} ou o coordenador podem editar">Editar (bloqueado)</button>`}
      <button class="btn" onclick="recadoTo('${m.id}')">Recado</button>
    </div>`;
}
function filterLinha(l){

let formLinhas=new Set(), formResultados=[], formPrazos=[], formFoto='';
let formHub=false, formHubMotivo='', formVinculos=[];
function openMember(id){
  if(id && !canEditMember(id)){ toast('Só '+memberById(id).nome+' ou o coordenador podem editar este cadastro.'); return; }
  if(!id && !isAdmin){ toast('Só o coordenador pode cadastrar novos membros.'); return; }
  editingId=id||null;
  const m = id? state.members.find(x=>x.id===id): null;
  $('#memberTitle').textContent = m? 'Editar aluno':'Novo aluno';
  $('#deleteMember').style.display = (m && isAdmin && !m.protegido)? 'inline-flex':'none';
  $('#selfEditNote').style.display = (m && isMe(id) && !isAdmin) ? 'block':'none';
  $('#mNome').value=m?m.nome:''; $('#mNivel').value=m?m.nivel:'doutorado';
  $('#mPrograma').value=m?m.programa:''; $('#mStatus').value=m?m.status:'ativo';
  $('#mBolsista').checked=!!m?.bolsista; $('#mBolsaModalidade').value=m?m.bolsaModalidade||'':''; $('#mBolsaInicio').value=m?m.bolsaInicio||'':''; $('#mBolsaFim').value=m?m.bolsaFim||'':'';
  $('#mOrigem').value=m?m.origem||'':''; $('#mTelefone').value=m?m.telefone||'':'';
  $('#mTema').value=m?m.tema:''; $('#mPlataforma').value=m?m.plataforma||'':''; $('#mDrive').value=m?m.driveLink||'':'';
  $('#mNotas').value=m?m.notas||'':'';
  const lockField=(el,locked)=>{el.disabled=locked};
  // não-admin editando o próprio: pode mexer em foto, tema, plataforma, resultados, prazos, notas — não em nível/programa/status
  const restrict = m && isMe(id) && !isAdmin;
  ['mNivel','mPrograma','mStatus','mOrigem'].forEach(k=>lockField($('#'+k),restrict)); ['mBolsista','mBolsaModalidade','mBolsaInicio','mBolsaFim'].forEach(k=>lockField($('#'+k),!isAdmin));
  formLinhas=new Set(m?m.linhas:[]);
  formResultados=m?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(m.resultados||[])):[];
  formPrazos=m?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(m.prazos||[])):[];
  formFoto=m?m.foto||'':'';
  formHub=m?!!m.hub:false; formHubMotivo=m?m.hubMotivo||'':'';
  formVinculos=m?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(m.vinculos||[])):[];
  $('#mFoto').value=formFoto.startsWith('data:')?'':formFoto;
  $('#mHub').checked=formHub;
  $('#mHubMotivo').style.display=formHub?'block':'none';
  $('#mHubMotivo').value=formHubMotivo;
  $('#vincSearch').value='';
  renderLinhasPick(); renderRepList(); renderPrazoList(); renderPhotoPrev(); renderVinculosPick(); toggleScholarFields();
  openOverlay('memberOverlay');
}

  state.linhas.forEach(l=>{
    const b=document.createElement('button'); b.type='button'; b.className='lp'+(formLinhas.has(l)?' on':''); b.textContent=l;
    b.onclick=()=>{formLinhas.has(l)?formLinhas.delete(l):formLinhas.add(l);b.classList.toggle('on')};
    el.appendChild(b);
  });
}

}

  el.querySelectorAll('[data-del]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=e=>{formPrazos.splice(+e.target.dataset.del,1);renderPrazoList()});
}

function renderVincPick(){

  options.forEach(o=>{
    const on=formVinculos.some(v=>v.id===o.id);
    const b=document.createElement('button'); b.type='button'; b.className='lp'+(on?' on':''

  if(!confirm('Excluir este aluno da rede? O perfil e o vínculo de acesso serão removidos. Esta ação não pode ser desfeita.'))return;
  try{
    const idToDelete=editingId, f=FB(), linked=(state.users||[]).filter(u=>u.memberId===idToDelete);
    const batch=f.writeBatch(window.db); batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',idToDelete)); batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_member_claims',idToDelete)); linked.forEach(u=>batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_users',u.uid))); await batch.commit();
    closeOverlay('memberOverlay'); if(selectedId===idToDelete)closeDetail();
    toast('Aluno excluído da rede e acesso desvinculado.');
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); toast('Erro ao remover.'); }
}

/* ============ GERENCIAR PESSOAS ============ */
function renderPeopleManager(){
  if(!isAdmin || !$('#peopleList')) return;
  const q=($('#peopleSearch')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const items=[...state.members].filter(m=>!q || [m.nome,m.programa,m.status,m.origem].some(v=>(v||'').toLowerCase().includes(q))).sort((a,b)=>(a.nome||'').localeCompare(b.nome||''));
  $('#peopleList').innerHTML=items.map(m=>{const nv=NIVEIS[m.nivel]||{color:'#607d8b',short:m.nivel||''};return `<div class="people-row"><div class="p-av" style="background:${nv.color}">${m.foto?`<img src="${esc(m.foto)}" alt="">`:initials(m.nome)}</div><div><div class="p-name">${esc(m.nome)}</div><div class="p-meta">${esc(nv.short)}${m.programa?' · '+esc(m.programa):''}${m.status==='egresso'?' · Egresso':''}${m.bolsista?' · 🎓 Bolsista':''}</div></div><div class="p-actions"><button type="button" class="btn" onclick="editPersonFromManager('${m.id}')">Editar / vínculos</button>${!m.protegido?`<button type="button" class="btn danger" onclick="removePersonFromManager('${m.id}')">Excluir aluno</button>`:''}</div></div>`}).join('') || '<div class="crono-empty">Nenhuma pessoa encontrada.</div>';
}
function openPeopleManager(){if(!isAdmin)return;renderPeopleManager();openOverlay('peopleOverlay')}
function editPersonFromManager(id){closeOverlay('peopleOverlay');openMember(id)}
async function removePersonFromManager(id){
  if(!isAdmin)return; const m=memberById(id); if(!m||m.protegido)return;
  if(!confirm(`Excluir ${m.nome} da rede? O perfil e o vínculo de acesso também serão removidos.`))return;
  try{
    const f=FB(), linked=(state.users||[]).filter(u=>u.memberId===id);
    const batch=f.writeBatch(window.db);
    batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id));
    batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_member_claims',id));
    linked.forEach(u=>batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_users',u.uid)));
    await batch.commit();
    toast('Aluno excluído da rede.'); renderPeopleManager();
  }catch(e){console.error(e);toast(e?.code==='permission-denied'?'Permissão negada pelas regras do Firestore.':'Erro ao retirar pessoa.')}
}

/* ============ VISUALIZADOR DE ARQUIVOS ============ */
let previewObjectUrl='', previewWorkbook=null;
function inferFileExt(url,name=''){
  const n=(name||'').split('?')[0].toLowerCase(); const m=n.match(/\.([a-z0-9]{2,6})$/); if(m)return m[1];
  try{const d=decodeURIComponent(new URL(url).pathname).toLowerCase();const x=d.match(/\.([a-z0-9]{2,6})$/);return x?x[1]:''}catch(e){return ''}
}
function sanitizePreviewHtml(html){
  const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString('<div>'+html+'</div>','text/html');
  doc.querySelectorAll('script,style,iframe,object,embed,form').forEach(n=>n.remove());
  doc.querySelectorAll('*').forEach(el=>{
    [...el.attributes].forEach(a=>{const n=a.name.toLowerCase(),v=(a.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();if(n.startsWith('on')||(n==='href'&&v.startsWith('javascript:')))el.removeAttribute(a.name)});
  });
  return doc.body.firstElementChild?.innerHTML||'';
}
async function previewFetch(url){
  const r=await fetch(url,{credentials:'omit',cache:'no-store'});
  if(!r.ok) throw new Error('Não foi possível carregar o arquivo ('+r.status+').');
  return r.arrayBuffer();
}
function onlineViewerHtml(url,name='',service='google'){
  const safeUrl=encodeURIComponent(url), safeName=esc(name||'Arquivo');
  const src=service==='office'
    ? `https://view.officeapps.live.com/op/embed.aspx?src=${safeUrl}`
    : `https://docs.google.com/gview?embedded=1&url=${safeUrl}`;
  return `<div class="online-preview-wrap"><div class="online-preview-note"><span>Visualização online de <b>${safeName}</b></span><div><button class="btn" type="button" onclick="switchOnlineViewer('google')">Google</button><button class="btn" type="button" onclick="switchOnlineViewer('office')">Microsoft</button></div></div><iframe class="file-preview-frame" src="${src}" title="${safeName}" referrerpolicy="no-referrer"></iframe></div>`;
}
let currentPreviewUrl='', currentPreviewName='';
function switchOnlineViewer(service='google'){
  const body=$('#filePreviewBody'); if(!body||!currentPreviewUrl)return;
  body.innerHTML=onlineViewerHtml(currentPreviewUrl,currentPreviewName,service);
}
function showWorkbookSheet(i){
  if(!previewWorkbook||!window.XLSX)return;
  const names=previewWorkbook.SheetNames||[]; i=Math.max(0,Math.min(names.length-1,+i||0));
  document.querySelectorAll('#excelTabs button').forEach((b,j)=>b.classList.toggle('on',j===i));
  const ws=previewWorkbook.Sheets[names[i]], html=window.XLSX.utils.sheet_to_html(ws,{id:'excelTable'});
  const el=$('#excelSheet'); if(el) el.innerHTML=sanitizePreviewHtml(html);
}
function externalViewerUrl(url,ext=''){
  const e=String(ext||'').toLowerCase(),safe=encodeURIComponent(url);
  if(['doc','docx','ppt','pptx','xls','xlsx','rtf','odt','ods','odp'].includes(e))return `https://view.officeapps.live.com/op/view.aspx?src=${safe}`;
  if(e==='pdf')return `https://docs.google.com/gview?embedded=0&url=${safe}`;
  return url;
}
async function renderPdfJs(viewUrl,name='PDF'){
  try{await ensureLib('pdfjs')}catch(e){throw new Error(e.message)}
  const body=$('#filePreviewBody');
  try{
    if(!window.pdfjsLib?.getDocument)throw new Error('Leitor PDF não carregou. Verifique sua conexão e tente novamente.');
    window.pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc='https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';
    body.innerHTML='<div class="preview-loading">Lendo PDF com o visualizador interno…</div>';
    const task=window.pdfjsLib.getDocument({url:viewUrl,withCredentials:false});
    const pdf=await task.promise;
    const host=document.createElement('div');host.className='pdfjs-preview';
    const bar=document.createElement('div');bar.className='pdfjs-toolbar';bar.textContent=`PDF · ${pdf.numPages} página${pdf.numPages===1?'':'s'} · visualização interna`;
    host.appendChild(bar);body.innerHTML='';body.appendChild(host);
    const maxCssWidth=Math.min(900,Math.max(280,(body.clientWidth||window.innerWidth)-36));
    const dpr=Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio||1,1.6);

document.addEventListener('touchstart',e=>{
  if(window.innerWidth>760 || !e.touches?.length)return;
  const t=e.touches[0];
  if(t.clientY<=120){mobileSwipeStartY=t.clientY;mobileSwipeStartX=t.clientX}else{mobileSwipeStartY=null}
},{passive:true});
document.addEventListener('touchend',e=>{
  if(mobileSwipeStartY==null || window.innerWidth>760)return;
  const t=e.changedTouches?.[0]; if(!t)return;
  const dy=t.clientY-mobileSwipeStartY, dx=Math.abs(t.clientX-mobileSwipeStartX);
  if(dy>34 && dx<90)showMobileTabs();
  mobileSwipeStartY=null;
},{passive:true});

function renderVincMotivos(){
  const el=$('#vincMotivos'); el.innerHTML='';
  if(!formVinculos.length) return;
  formVinculos.forEach((v,i)=>{
    const m=memberById(v.id);
    const row=document.createElement('div'); row.className='vinc-row';
    const nm=document.createElement('span'); nm.textContent=m?m.nome:'—';
    nm.style.cssText='flex:0 0 38%;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;color:var(--ink-2)';
    const inp=document.createElement('input'); inp.placeholder='Motivo (opcional)'; inp.value=v.motivo||''; inp.dataset.i=i;
    const del=document.createElement('button'); del.type='button'; del.className='icon-x'; del.textContent='×'; del.dataset.i=i;
    row.append(nm,inp,del); el.appendChild(row);
  });
  el.querySelectorAll('input').forEach(inp=>inp.oninput=e=>{formVinculos[+e.target.dataset.i].motivo=e.target.value});
  el.querySelectorAll('button.icon-x').forEach(b=>b.onclick=e=>{formVinculos.splice(+e.target.dataset.i,1);renderVinculosPick()});
}
function toggleScholarFields(){
  const on=$('#mBolsista')?.checked;
  document.querySelectorAll('.bolsa-field').forEach(el=>{el.style.opacity=on?'1':'.45'});
  ['mBolsaModalidade','mBolsaInicio','mBolsaFim'].forEach(k=>{const el=$('#'+k);if(el && isAdmin)el.disabled=!on});
}
function renderPhotoPrev(){
  const el=$('#photoPrev');
  if(formFoto){el.innerHTML=`<img src="${esc(formFoto)}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;border-radius:14px" onerror="this.parentNode.textContent='URL inválida'">`;}
  else el.textContent='sem foto';
}
async function saveMember(){
  const nome=$('#mNome').value.trim();
  if(!nome){toast('Informe o nome.');return}
  if(editingId && !canEditMember(editingId)){toast('Sem permissão para editar este cadastro.');return}
  if(!editingId && !isAdmin){toast('Só o coordenador pode cadastrar novos membros.');return}

  let fotoUrl=formFoto;
  if(formFoto && formFoto.startsWith('data:')){
    try{
      toast('Enviando foto…');
      const {url}=await fbUploadImage('membro_'+(editingId||nome.replace(/\s+/g,'_')),formFoto);
      fotoUrl=url;
    }catch(e){ console.error(e); toast('Falha ao enviar a foto. Salvando sem imagem.'); fotoUrl=''; }
  }

  const data={
    nome, nivel:$('#mNivel').value, programa:$('#mPrograma').value.trim(), status:$('#mStatus').value,
    bolsista:$('#mBolsista').checked, bolsaModalidade:$('#mBolsaModalidade').value.trim(), bolsaInicio:$('#mBolsaInicio').value, bolsaFim:$('#mBolsaFim').value,
    origem:$('#mOrigem').value.trim(), telefone:$('#mTelefone').value.trim(),
    tema:$('#mTema').value.trim(), plataforma:$('#mPlataforma').value.trim(), driveLink:$('#mDrive').value.trim(), notas:$('#mNotas').value.trim(),
    linhas:[...formLinhas], resultados:formResultados.filter(r=>r.titulo||r.url), prazos:formPrazos.filter(p=>p.titulo), foto:fotoUrl,
    hub:$('#mHub').checked, hubMotivo:$('#mHubMotivo').value.trim(),
    vinculos:formVinculos.filter(v=>v.id)
  };
  try{
    if(editingId){ await fbSetMember(editingId,data); }
    else { const id=uid(); await fbSetMember(id,{id,...data,createdAt:FB().serverTimestamp()}); }
    closeOverlay('memberOverlay'); toast(editingId?'Aluno atualizado.':'Aluno cadastrado.');
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); toast('Erro ao salvar. Verifique a conexão.'); }
}
async function deleteMember(){
  if(!editingId || !isAdmin)return;
  const m=memberById(editingId);
  if(m && m.protegido){ toast('O perfil do coordenador não pode ser removido por aqui.'); return; }
  if(!confirm('Excluir este aluno da rede? O perfil e o vínculo de acesso serão removidos. Esta ação não pode ser desfeita.'))return;
  try{
    const idToDelete=editingId, f=FB(), linked=(state.users||[]).filter(u=>u.memberId===idToDelete);
    const batch=f.writeBatch(window.db); batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',idToDelete)); batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_member_claims',idToDelete)); linked.forEach(u=>batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_users',u.uid))); await batch.commit();
    closeOverlay('memberOverlay'); if(selectedId===idToDelete)closeDetail();
    toast('Aluno excluído da rede e acesso desvinculado.');
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); toast('Erro ao remover.'); }
}

/* ============ GERENCIAR PESSOAS ============ */
function renderPeopleManager(){
  if(!isAdmin || !$('#peopleList')) return;
  const q=($('#peopleSearch')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const items=[...state.members].filter(m=>!q || [m.nome,m.programa,m.status,m.origem].some(v=>(v||'').toLowerCase().includes(q))).sort((a,b)=>(a.nome||'').localeCompare(b.nome||''));
  $('#peopleList').innerHTML=items.map(m=>{const nv=NIVEIS[m.nivel]||{color:'#607d8b',short:m.nivel||''};return `<div class="people-row"><div class="p-av" style="background:${nv.color}">${m.foto?`<img src="${esc(m.foto)}" alt="">`:initials(m.nome)}</div><div><div class="p-name">${esc(m.nome)}</div><div class="p-meta">${esc(nv.short)}${m.programa?' · '+esc(m.programa):''}${m.status==='egresso'?' · Egresso':''}${m.bolsista?' · 🎓 Bolsista':''}</div></div><div class="p-actions"><button type="button" class="btn" onclick="editPersonFromManager('${m.id}')">Editar / vínculos</button>${!m.protegido?`<button type="button" class="btn danger" onclick="removePersonFromManager('${m.id}')">Excluir aluno</button>`:''}</div></div>`}).join('') || '<div class="crono-empty">Nenhuma pessoa encontrada.</div>';
}
function openPeopleManager(){if(!isAdmin)return;renderPeopleManager();openOverlay('peopleOverlay')}
function editPersonFromManager(id){closeOverlay('peopleOverlay');openMember(id)}
async function removePersonFromManager(id){
  if(!isAdmin)return; const m=memberById(id); if(!m||m.protegido)return;
  if(!confirm(`Excluir ${m.nome} da rede? O perfil e o vínculo de acesso também serão removidos.`))return;
  try{
    const f=FB(), linked=(state.users||[]).filter(u=>u.memberId===id);
    const batch=f.writeBatch(window.db);
    batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id));
    batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_member_claims',id));
    linked.forEach(u=>batch.delete(f.doc(window.db,'rede_users',u.uid)));
    await batch.commit();
    toast('Aluno excluído da rede.'); renderPeopleManager();
  }catch(e){console.error(e);toast(e?.code==='permission-denied'?'Permissão negada pelas regras do Firestore.':'Erro ao retirar pessoa.')}
}

let previewObjectUrl='', previewWorkbook=null;
function inferFileExt(url,name=''){
  const n=(name||'').split('?')[0].toLowerCase(); const m=n.match(/\.([a-z0-9]{2,6})$/); if(m)return m[1];
  try{const d=decodeURIComponent(new URL(url).pathname).toLowerCase();const x=d.match(/\.([a-z0-9]{2,6})$/);return x?x[1]:''}catch(e){return ''}
}

const mobileTabs=$('#tabs');
let mobileTabsTimer=null;
function showMobileTabs(){
  if(!mobileTabs || window.innerWidth>760)return;
  mobileTabs.classList.add('mobile-open');
  clearTimeout(mobileTabsTimer);
  mobileTabsTimer=setTimeout(()=>mobileTabs.classList.remove('mobile-open'),5200);
}
function hideMobileTabs(){ if(mobileTabs) mobileTabs.classList.remove('mobile-open'); }
if($('#mobileNavHandle')) $('#mobileNavHandle').onclick=()=>mobileTabs?.classList.contains('mobile-open')?hideMobileTabs():showMobileTabs();
if(mobileTabs) mobileTabs.addEventListener('click',()=>setTimeout(hideMobileTabs,180));

/* gesto: deslizar para baixo na faixa superior mostra as abas */
let mobileSwipeStartY=null, mobileSwipeStartX=null;
document.addEventListener('touchstart',e=>{
  if(window.innerWidth>760 || !e.touches?.length)return;
  const t=e.touches[0];
  if(t.clientY<=120){mobileSwipeStartY=t.clientY;mobileSwipeStartX=t.clientX}else{mobileSwipeStartY=null}
},{passive:true});
document.addEventListener('touchend',e=>{
  if(mobileSwipeStartY==null || window.innerWidth>760)return;
  const t=e.changedTouches?.[0]; if(!t)return;
  const dy=t.clientY-mobileSwipeStartY, dx=Math.abs(t.clientX-mobileSwipeStartX);
  if(dy>34 && dx<90)showMobileTabs();
  mobileSwipeStartY=null;
},{passive:true});

<script data-src="repository-delete-dedupe-p65.js">
/* Carbonautas P65 · corrige duplicação infinita do botão Excluir nas pastas */
(function(){
'use strict';
let scheduled=false;
function packageId(card){
  const el=card.querySelector('[onclick*="openRepositoryPackage"]');
  const m=String(el?.getAttribute('onclick')||'').match(/openRepositoryPackage\('([^']+)'\)/);
  return m?.[1]||'';
}
function fixDeleteButtons(){
  document.querySelectorAll('#repoPackageList .repo-package-card').forEach(card=>{
    const area=card.querySelector('.repo-package-actions');
    if(!area)return;
    const id=packageId(card);
    if(!id)return;
    const buttons=[...area.querySelectorAll('button.p64-delete,button[onclick*="deleteRepositoryPackage"]')];
    if(!buttons.length)return;
    const keep=buttons.find(b=>String(b.getAttribute('onclick')||'').includes('deleteRepositoryPackage'))||buttons[0];
    buttons.forEach(b=>{if(b!==keep)b.remove()});
    keep.classList.remove('mini-x');
    keep.classList.add('btn','p64-delete');
    keep.textContent='🗑 Excluir';
    keep.title='Excluir pasta';
    keep.setAttribute('onclick',`deleteRepositoryPackage(${JSON.stringify(id)})`);
    keep.dataset.p65SingleDelete='1';
  });
}
function schedule(){
  if(scheduled)return;
  scheduled=true;
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{scheduled=false;fixDeleteButtons()});
}
function boot(){
  fixDeleteButtons();
  const root=document.getElementById('repoPackageList')||document.body;
  new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  setTimeout(fixDeleteButtons,300);
  setTimeout(fixDeleteButtons,1200);
  console.info('Carbonautas P65 · exclusão única por pasta carregada');
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

</script>

  }finally{scanBusy=false}
}
async function canDeletePackageP64(pkg){const u=window.auth?.currentUser,me=await myProfile();if(!u||!pkg)return false;if(pkg.createdByUid===u.uid||pkg.ownerMemberId===me?.memberId)return true;return (await isCoordinator())&&pkg.accessMode==='group'&&pkg.personalRepo!==true}
async function deletePackageP64(id){
  try{const pkg=await getPackage(id);if(!pkg)return toast('A pasta não existe mais.');if(!(await canDeletePackageP64(pkg)))return toast('Você não pode excluir esta pasta.');if(!(await confirmDeleteFolder(pkg.title||'esta pasta')))return;const F=f(),pref=F.doc(window.db,'rede_repository_packages',id),snap=await F.getDocs(F.collection(pref,'files'));for(const d of snap.docs){const x=d.data();if(x.storagePath&&F.deleteObject&&F.sRef&&window.storage){try{await F.deleteObject(F.sRef(window.storage,x.storagePath))}catch(e){console.warn('P64 storage',e)}}await F.deleteDoc(d.ref)}await F.deleteDoc(pref);try{if(typeof window.closeOverlay==='function')window.closeOverlay('repoPackageOverlay')}catch(e){}toast('Pasta excluída.')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast(e?.code==='permission-denied'?'O Firestore ainda não permite esta exclusão. Publique as regras P64.':(e.message||'Não foi possível excluir a pasta.'))}
}

function patchGlobals(){
  if(patched)return;patched=true;
  window.messagePublicationOwner=id=>openPublicationChat(id);window.openRepoFileConversationPublication=id=>openPublicationChat(id);window.openReviewConversation=id=>openPublicationChat(id);window.openRepoFileConversationPackage=(packageId,fileId)=>openPackageChat(packageId,fileId);window.openRepositoryPrivateChat=openPublicationChat;window.openPrivateChatWithContext=openPrivateChatWithContext;window.deleteRepositoryPackage=deletePackageP64;
  const originalOpen=window.openPrivateThread;if(typeof originalOpen==='function'&&!originalOpen.__p64){const wrapped=function(id){const out=originalOpen.apply(this,arguments);watchContextThread(id);setTimeout(decorateContextMessages,80);return out};wrapped.__p64=true;window.openPrivateThread=wrapped}
}
function boot(){injectCss();patchGlobals();decorateRepository();setInterval(()=>{decorateRepository();decorateContextMessages()},900);console.info('Carbonautas',VERSION,BUILD,'repositório → privadas 1:1 + exclusão segura carregados')}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

<script data-src="repository-delete-dedupe-p65.js">
/* Carbonautas P65 · corrige duplicação infinita do botão Excluir nas pastas */
(function(){
'use strict';
let scheduled=false;
function packageId(card){
  const el=card.querySelector('[onclick*="openRepositoryPackage"]');
  const m=String(el?.getAttribute('onclick')||'').match(/openRepositoryPackage\('([^']+)'\)/);
  return m?.[1]||'';
}
function fixDeleteButtons(){
  document.querySelectorAll('#repoPackageList .repo-package-card').forEach(card=>{
    const area=card.querySelector('.repo-package-actions');
    if(!area)return;
    const id=packageId(card);
    if(!id)return;
    const buttons=[...area.querySelectorAll('button.p64-delete,button[onclick*="deleteRepositoryPackage"]')];
    if(!buttons.length)return;
    const keep=buttons.find(b=>String(b.getAttribute('onclick')||'').includes('deleteRepositoryPackage'))||buttons[0];
    buttons.forEach(b=>{if(b!==keep)b.remove()});
    keep.classList.remove('mini-x');
    keep.classList.add('btn','p64-delete');
    keep.textContent='🗑 Excluir';
    keep.title='Excluir pasta';
    keep.setAttribute('onclick',`deleteRepositoryPackage(${JSON.stringify(id)})`);
    keep.dataset.p65SingleDelete='1';
  });
}
function schedule(){
  if(scheduled)return;
  scheduled=true;
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{scheduled=false;fixDeleteButtons()});
}
function boot(){
  fixDeleteButtons();
  const root=document.getElementById('repoPackageList')||document.body;
  new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  setTimeout(fixDeleteButtons,300);
  setTimeout(fixDeleteButtons,1200);
  console.info('Carbonautas P65 · exclusão única por pasta carregada');
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();

</script>

function fmtTime(ts){
  if(!ts) return '';
  const d=new Date(ts), now=new Date();
  const sameDay=d.toDateString()===now.toDateString();
  return sameDay ? d.toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})
                 : d.toLocaleDateString('pt-BR',{day:'2-digit',month:'2-digit'})+' '+d.toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
}
function dayLabel(ts){
  const d=new Date(ts), now=new Date();
  const y=new Date(now); y.setDate(now.getDate()-1);
  if(d.toDateString()===now.toDateString()) return 'Hoje';
  if(d.toDateString()===y.toDateString()) return 'Ontem';
  return d.toLocaleDateString('pt-BR',{day:'2-digit',month:'long',year:'numeric'});
}

let dbReady = false;
function waitFirebase(){
  return new Promise(res=>{
    if(window.db && window.fbFns){dbReady=true;return res()}
    window.addEventListener('firebase-ready',()=>{dbReady=true;res()},{once:true});
  });
}

async function bootstrap(){
  await waitFirebase();
  if(!authUser || !authProfile) return;
  const f=FB();
  try{
    clearSubscriptions();
    let memSnap = await f.getDocs(f.collection(window.db,'rede_members'));
    if(memSnap.empty){
      if(!isAdmin) throw new Error('A rede ainda precisa ser inicializada pelo coordenador.');
      $('#loadingMsg').textContent='Preparando a rede pela primeira vez…';
      const seedIds={}; SEED.forEach(x=>{seedIds[x.nome]=slugify(x.nome)});
      for(const x of SEED){
        const id=seedIds[x.nome], hub=!!SEED_HUBS[x.nome], hubMotivo=SEED_HUBS[x.nome]||'';
        const vinculos=SEED_VINCULOS.filter(v=>v.de===x.nome && seedIds[v.para]).map(v=>({id:seedIds[v.para],motivo:v.motivo}));
        await f.setDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id),{id,foto:'',telefone:'',resultados:[],prazos:[],notas:'',hub,hubMotivo,vinculos,createdAt:f.serverTimestamp(),...x});
      }
      memSnap = await f.getDocs(f.collection(window.db,'rede_members'));
    } else if(isAdmin) {
      const existing=memSnap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id})), byName={}; existing.forEach(m=>{byName[m.nome]=m});
      if(!existing.some(m=>m.nivel==='coord')){
        const x=SEED.find(v=>v.nivel==='coord'); if(x){const id=slugify(x.nome); await f.setDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id),{id,foto:'',telefone:'',resultados:[],prazos:[],notas:'',hub:false,hubMotivo:'',vinculos:[],createdAt:f.serverTimestamp(),...x}); byName[x.nome]={id,...x}; memSnap=await f.getDocs(f.collection(window.db,'rede_members'));}
      }
      // Migração apenas de campos ausentes. Vínculos não são mais restaurados automaticamente:
      // depois da primeira inicialização, a rede passa a ser totalmente editável pelo coordenador.
      for(const [nome,motivo] of Object.entries(SEED_HUBS)){const m=byName[nome]; if(m&&m.hub===undefined) await f.setDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',m.id),{hub:true,hubMotivo:motivo},{merge:true});}
    }
    state.members=memSnap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));
    state.members.forEach(m=>{m.linhas=m.linhas||[];m.resultados=m.resultados||[];m.prazos=m.prazos||[];m.vinculos=m.vinculos||[];m.hub=!!m.hub;m.bolsista=!!m.bolsista});
    const linkedNow=resolveCurrentMember();
    if(linkedNow && (linkedNow.nivel==='coord' || linkedNow.protegido===true || COORDINATOR_MEMBER_IDS.has(linkedNow.id))) isAdmin=true;
    if(isAdmin){
      const linked=resolveCurrentMember();
      if(linked && linked.id!==myId){
        try{
          await f.setDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_users',authUser.uid),{memberId:linked.id,nome:linked.nome||authProfile.nome||'',email:authUser.email||'',role:'coordinator',updatedAt:f.serverTimestamp()},{merge:true});
          myId=linked.id; authProfile.memberId=linked.id; authProfile.nome=linked.nome||authProfile.nome;
        }catch(e){console.warn('Não foi possível autorreparar o vínculo',e)}
      }
    }
    const setRef=f.doc(window.db,'rede_settings','app'), setSnap=await f.getDoc(setRef);
    if(setSnap.exists()){
      state.settings={...state.settings,...setSnap.data()};
      if(!Array.isArray(state.settings.linhas)||!state.settings.linhas.length){state.settings.linhas=DEFAULT_LINHAS.slice(); if(isAdmin) await f.setDoc(setRef,{linhas:state.settings.linhas},{merge:true});}
    } else {
      state.settings.linhas=DEFAULT_LINHAS.slice();
      if(isAdmin) await f.setDoc(setRef,{groupLink:'',linhas:state.settings.linhas});
    }
    state.linhas=state.settings.linhas;
    // Recursos privados não podem bloquear a entrada na plataforma.
    // Inicializam em segundo plano depois que o núcleo da conta já foi validado.
    ensurePrivateDirectoryEntry().catch(e=>console.warn('Diretório privado indisponível:',e));
    ensurePersonalRepositoryRoot().catch(e=>console.warn('Repositório privado indisponível:',e));
    subscribeAll();
    bootstrapped=true;
    $('#authGate').classList.add('off');
    $('#loading').classList.add('off');
    refreshIdentityUI();
  }catch(e){
    console.error(e);
    $('#loading').classList.add('off');
    $('#authMsg').textContent=e?.message||'Não foi possível abrir a plataforma.';
    $('#authGate').classList.remove('off');
  }
}

}

  const sameDay=d.toDateString()===now.toDateString();
  return sameDay ? d.toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})
                 : d.toLocaleDateString('pt-BR',{day:'2-digit',month:'2-digit'})+' '+d.toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
}
function dayLabel(ts){
  const d=new Date(ts), now=new Date();
  const y=new Date(now); y.setDate(now.getDate()-1);
  if(d.toDateString()===now.toDateString()) return 'Hoje';
  if(d.toDateString()===y.toDateString()) return 'Ontem';
  return d.toLocaleDateString('pt-BR',{day:'2-digit',month:'long',year:'numeric'});
}
function memberById(id){return state.members.find(m=>m.id===id)}
function memberForAuthor(id,name=''){const direct=memberById(id);const same=state.members.filter(m=>name&&m.nome===name);if(direct?.foto)return direct;return same.find(m=>m.foto)||direct||same[0]||null}

/* ============ FIREBASE LAYER ============ */
let dbReady = false;
function waitFirebase(){
  return new Promise(res=>{
    if(window.db && window.fbFns){dbReady=true;return res()}
    window.addEventListener('firebase-ready',()=>{dbReady=true;res()},{once:true});
  });
}

async function bootstrap(){
  await waitFirebase();
  if(!authUser || !authProfile) return;
  const f=FB();
  try{
    clearSubscriptions();
    let memSnap = await f.getDocs(f.collection(window.db,'rede_members'));
    if(memSnap.empty){
      if(!isAdmin) throw new Error('A rede ainda precisa ser inicializada pelo coordenador.');
      $('#loadingMsg').textContent='Preparando a rede pela primeira vez…';
      const seedIds={}; SEED.forEach(x=>{seedIds[x.nome]=slugify(x.nome)});
      for(const x of SEED){
        const id=seedIds[x.nome], hub=!!SEED_HUBS[x.nome], hubMotivo=SEED_HUBS[x.nome]||'';
        const vinculos=SEED_VINCULOS.filter(v=>v.de===x.nome && seedIds[v.para]).map(v=>({id:seedIds[v.para],motivo:v.motivo}));
        await f.setDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id),{id,foto:'',telefone:'',resultados:[],prazos:[],notas:'',hub,hubMotivo,vinculos,createdAt:f.serverTimestamp(),...x});
      }
      memSnap = await f.getDocs(f.collection(window.db,'rede_members'));
    } else if(isAdmin) {
      const existing=memSnap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id})), byName={}; existing.forEach(m=>{byName[m.nome]=m});
      if(!existing.some(m=>m.nivel==='coord')){
        const x=SEED.find(v=>v.nivel==='coord'); if(x){const id=slugify(x.nome); await f.setDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id),{id,foto:'',telefone:'',resultados:[],prazos:[],notas:'',hub:false,hubMotivo:'',vinculos:[],createdAt:f.serverTimestamp(),...x}); byName[x.nome]={id,...x}; memSnap=await f.getDocs(f.collection(window.db,'rede_members'));}
      }
      // Migração apenas de campos ausentes. Vínculos não são mais restaurados automaticamente:
      // depois da primeira inicialização, a rede passa a ser totalmente editável pelo coordenador.
      for(const [nome,motivo] of Object.entries(SEED_HUBS)){const m=byName[nome]; if(m&&m.hub===undefined) await f.setDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',m.id),{hub:true,hubMotivo:motivo},{merge:true});}
    }
    state.members=memSnap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));
    state.members.forEach(m=>{m.linhas=m.linhas||[];m.resultados=m.resultados||[];m.prazos=m.prazos||[];m.vinculos=m.vinculos||[];m.hub=!!m.hub;m.bolsista=!!m.bolsista});
    const linkedNow=resolveCurrentMember();
    if(linkedNow && (linkedNow.nivel==='coord' || linkedNow.protegido===true || COORDINATOR_MEMBER_IDS.has(linkedNow.id))) isAdmin=true;
    if(isAdmin){
      const linked=resolveCurrentMember();
      if(linked && linked.id!==myId){
        try{
          await f.setDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_users',authUser.uid),{memberId:linked.id,nome:linked.nome||authProfile.nome||'',email:authUser.email||'',role:'coordinator',updatedAt:f.serverTimestamp()},{merge:true});
          myId=linked.id; authProfile.memberId=linked.id; authProfile.nome=linked.nome||authProfile.nome;
        }catch(e){console.warn('Não foi possível autorreparar o vínculo',e)}
      }
    }
    const setRef=f.doc(window.db,'rede_settings','app'), setSnap=await f.getDoc(setRef);
    if(setSnap.exists()){
      state.settings={...state.settings,...setSnap.data()};
      if(!Array.isArray(state.settings.linhas)||!state.settings.linhas.length){state.settings.linhas=DEFAULT_LINHAS.slice(); if(isAdmin) await f.setDoc(setRef,{linhas:state.settings.linhas},{merge:true});}
    } else {
      state.settings.linhas=DEFAULT_LINHAS.slice();
      if(isAdmin) await f.setDoc(setRef,{groupLink:'',linhas:state.settings.linhas});
    }
    state.linhas=state.settings.linhas;
    // Recursos privados não podem bloquear a entrada na plataforma.
    // Inicializam em segundo plano depois que o núcleo da conta já foi validado.
    ensurePrivateDirectoryEntry().catch(e=>console.warn('Diretório privado indisponível:',e));
    ensurePersonalRepositoryRoot().catch(e=>console.warn('Repositório privado indisponível:',e));
    subscribeAll();
    bootstrapped=true;
    $('#authGate').classList.add('off');
    $('#loading').classList.add('off');
    refreshIdentityUI();
  }catch(e){
    console.error(e);
    $('#loading').classList.add('off');
    $('#authMsg').textContent=e?.message||'Não foi possível abrir a plataforma.';
    $('#authGate').classList.remove('off');
  }
}


async function ensurePersonalRepositoryRoot(){
  if(!window.auth?.currentUser||!myId)return;
  const f=FB(),ref=f.doc(window.db,'rede_personal_repositories',myId),snap=await f.getDoc(ref);
  if(snap.exists())return;
  const m=memberById(myId),uid=window.auth.currentUser.uid;
  await f.setDoc(ref,{ownerUid:uid,ownerMemberId:myId,ownerName:m?.nome||myName||'',allowedMemberIds:[myId],collaboratorMemberIds:[],createdAt:f.serverTimestamp(),updatedAt:f.serverTimestamp()});
}

function clearSubscriptions(){subscriptions.forEach(fn=>{try{fn()}catch(e){}});subscriptions=[];if(privateMessageUnsub){try{privateMessageUnsub()}catch(e){}privateMessageUnsub=null;}privateMessagesState=[];privateActiveThreadId=null;}

function subscribeAll(){
  const f=FB();
  // SETTINGS (linhas de pesquisa, pin, link do grupo)
  subscriptions.push(f.onSnapshot(f.doc(window.db,'rede_settings','app'),snap=>{
    if(!snap.exists())return;
    const d=snap.data();
    state.settings={...state.settings,...d};
    if(Array.isArray(d.linhas) && d.linhas.length){
      state.linhas=d.linhas;
      if(currentView==='rede') renderAll();
    }
  }));
  // MEMBERS
  subscriptions.push(f.onSnapshot(f.collection(window.db,'rede_members'),snap=>{
    state.members=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));
    state.members.forEach(m=>{m.linhas=m.linhas||[];m.resultados=m.resultados||[];m.prazos=m.prazos||[];m.vinculos=m.vinculos||[];m.hub=!!m.hub;m.bolsista=!!m.bolsista});
    if(currentView==='painel') renderPainel();
    if(currentView==='rede') renderAll();
    if(currentView==='crono') renderCrono();
    updateAttentionBadge();
    refreshMemberDependentUI();
    if(currentView==='conversas') renderPrivateThreads();
    if($('#privateNewOverlay')?.classList.contains('open')) renderPrivatePeople();
    if(selectedId && memberById(selectedId)) renderDetail(); if(isAdmin && $('#peopleOverlay')?.classList.contains('on')) renderPeopleManager();
  }));
  // CHAT
  subscriptions.push(f.onSnapshot(f.query(f.collection(
-- pos=305898 --
R',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
}

    state.members.forEach(m=>{m.linhas=m.linhas||[];m.resultados=m.resultados||[];m.prazos=m.prazos||[];m.vinculos=m.vinculos||[];m.hub=!!m.hub;m.bolsista=!!m.bolsista});

    if(currentView==='rede') renderAll();
    if(currentView==='crono') renderCrono();
    updateAttentionBadge();
    refreshMemberDependentUI();

    if($('#privateNewOverlay')?.classList.contains('open')) renderPrivatePeople();
    if(selectedId && memberById(selectedId)) renderDetail(); if(isAdmin && $('#peopleOverlay')?.classList.contains('on')) renderPeopleManager();
  }));
  // CHAT
  subscriptions.push(f.onSnapshot(f.query(f.collection(window.db,'rede_chat'),f.orderBy('ts','asc')),snap=>{
    state.chat=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));
    if(currentView==='mural'){ renderMural(); markChatSeen(); }
    else updateMuralBadge();
    if(currentView==='mensagens'){ renderMensagens(); markQuickSeen(); }
    else updateQuickBadge();

/* ============ FIREBASE LAYER ============ */
let dbReady = false;
function waitFirebase(){
  return new Promise(res=>{
    if(window.db && window.fbFns){dbReady=true;return res()}
    window.addEventListener('firebase-ready',()=>{dbReady=true;res()},{once:true});
  });
}

async function bootstrap(){
  await waitFirebase();
  if(!authUser || !authProfile) return;
  const f=FB();
  try{
    clearSubscriptions();
    let memSnap = await f.getDocs(f.collection(window.db,'rede_members'));
    if(memSnap.empty){
      if(!isAdmin) throw new Error('A rede ainda precisa ser inicializada pelo coordenador.');
      $('#loadingMsg').textContent='Preparando a rede pela primeira vez…';
      const seedIds={}; SEED.forEach(x=>{seedIds[x.nome]=slugify(x.nome)});
      for(const x of SEED){
        const id=seedIds[x.nome], hub=!!SEED_HUBS[x.nome], hubMotivo=SEED_HUBS[x.nome]||'';
        const vinculos=SEED_VINCULOS.filter(v=>v.de===x.nome && seedIds[v.para]).map(v=>({id:seedIds[v.para],motivo:v.motivo}));
        await f.setDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id),{id,foto:'',telefone:'',resultados:[],prazos:[],notas:'',hub,hubMotivo,vinculos,createdAt:f.serverTimestamp(),...x});
      }
      memSnap = await f.getDocs(f.collection(window.db,'rede_members'));
    } else if(isAdmin) {
      const existing=memSnap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id})), byName={}; existing.forEach(m=>{byName[m.nome]=m});
      if(!existing.some(m=>m.nivel==='coord')){
        const x=SEED.find(v=>v.nivel==='coord'); if(x){const id=slugify(x.nome); await f.setDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',id),{id,foto:'',telefone:'',resultados:[],prazos:[],notas:'',hub:false,hubMotivo:'',vinculos:[],createdAt:f.serverTimestamp(),...x}); byName[x.nome]={id,...x}; memSnap=await f.getDocs(f.collection(window.db,'rede_members'));}
      }
      // Migração apenas de campos ausentes. Vínculos não são mais restaurados automaticamente:
      // depois da primeira inicialização, a rede passa a ser totalmente editável pelo coordenador.
      for(const [nome,motivo] of Object.entries(SEED_HUBS)){const m=byName[nome]; if(m&&m.hub===undefined) await f.setDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_members',m.id),{hub:true,hubMotivo:motivo},{merge:true});}
    }
    state.members=memSnap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));
    state.members.forEach(m=>{m.linhas=m.linhas||[];m.resultados=m.resultados||[];m.prazos=m.prazos||[];m.vinculos=m.vinculos||[];m.hub=!!m.hub;m.bolsista=!!m.bolsista});
    const linkedNow=resolveCurrentMember();
    if(linkedNow && (linkedNow.nivel==='coord' || linkedNow.protegido===true || COORDINATOR_MEMBER_IDS.has(linkedNow.id))) isAdmin=true;
    if(isAdmin){
      const linked=resolveCurrentMember();
      if(linked && linked.id!==myId){
        try{
          await f.setDoc(f.doc(window.db,'rede_users',authUser.uid),{memberId:linked.id,nome:linked.nome||authProfile.nome||'',email:authUser.email||'',role:'coordinator',updatedAt:f.serverTimestamp()},{merge:true});
          myId=linked.id; authProfile.memberId=linked.id; authProfile.nome=linked.nome||authProfile.nome;
        }catch(e){console.warn('Não foi possível autorreparar o vínculo',e)}
      }
    }
    const setRef=f.doc(window.db,'rede_settings','app'), setSnap=await f.getDoc(setRef);
    if(setSnap.exists()){
      state.settings={...state.settings,...setSnap.data()};
      if(!Array.isArray(state.settings.linhas)||!state.settings.linhas.length){state.settings.linhas=DEFAULT_LINHAS.slice(); if(isAdmin) await f.setDoc(setRef,{linhas:state.settings.linhas},{merge:true});}
    } else {
      state.settings.linhas=DEFAULT_LINHAS.slice();
      if(isAdmin) await f.setDoc(setRef,{groupLink:'',linhas:state.settings.linhas});
    }
    state.linhas=state.settings.linhas;
    // Recursos privados não podem bloquear a entrada na plataforma.
    // Inicializam em segundo plano depois que o núcleo da conta já foi validado.
    ensurePrivateDirectoryEntry().catch(e=>console.warn('Diretório privado indisponível:',e));
    ensurePersonalRepositoryRoot().catch(e=>console.warn('Repositório privado indisponível:',e));
    subscribeAll();
    bootstrapped=true;
    $('#authGate').classList.add('off');
    $('#loading').classList.add('off');
    refreshIdentityUI();
  }catch(e){
    console.error(e);
    $('#loading').classList.add('off');
    $('#authMsg').textContent=e?.message||'Não foi possível abrir a plataforma.';
    $('#authGate').classList.remove('off');
  }
}


async function ensurePersonalRepositoryRoot(){
  if(!window.auth?.currentUser||!myId)return;
  const f=FB(),ref=f.doc(window.db,'rede_personal_repositories',myId),snap=await f.getDoc(ref);
  if(snap.exists())return;
  const m=memberById(myId),uid=window.auth.currentUser.uid;
  await f.setDoc(ref,{ownerUid:uid,ownerMemberId:myId,ownerName:m?.nome||myName||'',allowedMemberIds:[myId],collaboratorMemberIds:[],createdAt:f.serverTimestamp(),updatedAt:f.serverTimestamp()});
}

function subscribeAll(){
  const f=FB();
  // SETTINGS (linhas de pesquisa, pin, link do grupo)
  subscriptions.push(f.onSnapshot(f.doc(window.db,'rede_settings','app'),snap=>{

    state.settings={...state.settings,...d};
    if(Array.isArray(d.linhas) && d.linhas.length){
      state.linhas=d.linhas;
      if(currentView==='rede') renderAll();
    }
  }));
  // MEMBERS
  subscriptions.push(f.onSnapshot(f.collection(window.db,'rede_members'),snap=>{
    state.members=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));
    state.members.forEach(m=>{m.linhas=m.linhas||[];m.resultados=m.resultados||[];m.prazos=m.prazos||[];m.vinculos=m.vinculos||[];m.hub=!!m.hub;m.bolsista=!!m.bolsista});
    if(currentView==='painel') renderPainel();
    if(currentView==='rede') renderAll();
    if(currentView==='crono') renderCrono();
    updateAttentionBadge();
    refreshMemberDependentUI();
    if(currentView==='conversas') renderPrivateThreads();
    if($('#privateNewOverlay')?.classList.contains('open')) renderPrivatePeople();
    if(selectedId && memberById(selectedId)) renderDetail(); if(isAdmin && $('#peopleOverlay')?.classList.contains('on')) renderPeopleManager();
  }));
  // CHAT
  subscriptions.push(f.onSnapshot(f.query(f.collection(window.db,'rede_chat'),f.orderBy('ts','asc')),snap=>{
    state.chat=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));
    if(currentView==='mural'){ renderMural(); markChatSeen(); }
    else updateMuralBadge();
    if(currentView==='mensagens'){ renderMensagens(); markQuickSeen(); }
    else updateQuickBadge();
  }));
  // SCHEDULE (eventos do grupo)
  subscriptions.push(f.onSnapshot(f.query(f.collection(window.db,'rede_schedule'),f.orderBy('data','asc')),snap=>{
    state.schedule=snap.docs.map(d=>({...d.data(),id:d.id}));
    if(currentView==='crono') renderCrono();
    if(currentView==='painel') renderPainel();
    updateCronoBadge();
  }));
  // AGENDA PRIVADA — somente o próprio UID consulta seus compromissos
  if(authUser?.uid){
    try{
      const privateQ=f.query(f.collection(window.db,'rede_private_schedule'),f.where('ownerUid','==',authUser.uid));
      subscriptions.push(f.onSnapshot(pr

    // Recursos privados não podem bloquear a entrada na plataforma.
    // Inicializam em segundo plano depois que o núcleo da conta já foi validado.
    ensurePrivateDirectoryEntry().catch(e=>console.warn('Diretório privado indisponível:',e));

    }catch(e){console.warn('Não consegui iniciar repositórios pe
